This question assesses your ability to use Al coding assistants (e.g., GitHub Copilot,
ChatGPT, Claude) as a practical aid during software development. Two Java code snippets
from the Course Registration System are provided below. For each scenario, you must craft
an effective Al prompt and then answer the follow-up tasks based on what the Al would
identify.  9
Important: A good Al prompt must include: (1) clear context about the system, (2) the
specific goal (review / find bug / fix), (3) the code itself, and (4) the expected output format.

+

application. It is intended to enroll a student in a course by (1) verifying the prerequisite is
satisfied, (2) confirming a seat is available, and (3) persisting the new registration record.
The code compiles without errors but contains THREE logical or semantic bugs.
Java

Question 6: Al in coding (1.0 points)
C
This question assesses your ability to use Al coding assistants (e.g., GitHub Copilot,
ChatGPT, Claude) as a practical aid during software development. Two Java code snippets
from the Course Registration System are provided below. For each scenario, you must craft
an effective Al prompt and then answer the follow-up tasks based on what the Al would
identify.  9

specific goal (review / find bug / fix), (3) the code itself, and (4) the expected output format.
